## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
SSH 到 westd-29999。改造 evaluate.py 的 MCQ logprob 评估为 batch 模式。这是拆分后的第一步（上次完整改造因 context 超时失败）。

## 目标
只改 `compute_choice_logprobs` 和 `evaluate_mcq` 函数，实现 batch logprob scoring。

## Step 1: 读取相关函数
```bash
grep -n "def compute_choice_logprobs\|def evaluate_mcq\|def evaluate_gsm8k" /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py
```
然后只读取 compute_choice_logprobs 和 evaluate_mcq 函数的代码（用 sed 提取行号范围）。

## Step 2: 理解当前实现
compute_choice_logprobs 函数：逐条对每个 choice 做前向传播，取 logits 计算概率。
evaluate_mcq 函数：for 循环逐条调用 compute_choice_logprobs。

## Step 3: 改造方案

### 新函数 batch_compute_choice_logprobs(model, tokenizer, batch_prompts, batch_choices, device)
- batch_prompts: list of str (每条的 prompt)
- batch_choices: list of list of str (每条的选项)
- 对每个 choice，构造 prompt+choice 拼接
- 收集所有 (prompt+choice) 组合 → left-pad tokenize → batch forward
- 提取每组的 logprob → 返回 list of (best_choice, logprobs_dict)

### 改造 evaluate_mcq
- 收集 batch_size 个 items
- 调用 batch_compute_choice_logprobs
- 处理结果

## Step 4: 实现
直接修改 /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001/evaluate.py（注意 --max-items 参数已经添加，不要删除）。

**关键约束**：
- `tokenizer.padding_side = "left"` 在 batch 时设置
- 如果 tokenizer 没有 pad_token，设为 eos_token
- batch forward 后，每条的 logprob 计算必须只看该条有效 token（不含 padding）
- 结果必须与逐条计算一致（greedy 选择相同）

## Step 5: 语法验证
```bash
cd /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001 && python -c "import ast; ast.parse(open('evaluate.py').read()); print('SYNTAX OK')"
```

## Step 6: 快速验证（用 7B d000 checkpoint，50 条 MMLU）
在空闲 GPU 上测试（先 nvidia-smi 看哪个空闲，可能 cuda:2 或 cuda:3）：
```bash
cd /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp001 && \
source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate && \
export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache && \
export HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com && \
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=&lt;FREE_GPU> python evaluate.py \
  --model-path /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B \
  --adapter-path /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Qwen2.5-7B_d000_s42/lora_adapter \
  --output-dir /tmp/eval_test_batch \
  --benchmarks mmlu \
  --batch-size 16 \
  --max-items 50 \
  --subsample-seed 42
```

对比基线（batch-size 1 = 逐条）：
```bash
CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=&lt;FREE_GPU> python evaluate.py \
  --model-path /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-7B \
  --adapter-path /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/Qwen2.5-7B_d000_s42/lora_adapter \
  --output-dir /tmp/eval_test_single \
  --benchmarks mmlu \
  --batch-size 1 \
  --max-items 50 \
  --subsample-seed 42
```

比较两个输出的准确率是否一致。

**输出要求**：改了哪些函数、关键 diff 摘要、验证结果（batch vs single 的 accuracy 是否一致）。

```json
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

If adapter_path is given, load base + LoRA adapter.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if adapter_path:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading base model: %s + adapter: %s\\\", model_path, adapter_path)\\n        from peft import PeftModel\\n        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n            adapter_path, cache_dir=cache_dir, trust_remote_code=True\\n        )\\n        base_model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path,\\n            cache_dir=cache_dir,\\n            torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n            trust_remote_code=True,\\n            device_map=\\\"auto\\\",\\n        )\\n        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(base_model, adapter_path)\\n        model = model.merge_and_unload()\\n    else:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading model: %s\\\", model_path)\\n        tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path, cache_dir=cache_dir, trust_remote_code=True\\n        )\\n        model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n            model_path,\\n            cache_dir=cache_dir,\\n            torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n            trust_remote_code=True,\\n            device_map=\\\"auto\\\",\\n        )\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    # Set chat template for models that don't have one (e.g. Llama-3.2 base)\\n    if tokenizer.chat_template is None:\\n        model_id = model_path.lower() if model_path else \\\"\\\"\\n        if \\\"llama\\\" in model_id:\\n            tokenizer.chat_template = LLAMA3_CHAT_TEMPLATE\\n            logger.info(\\\"Set Llama 3 chat template on tokenizer\\\")\\n\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    return model, tokenizer\\n\\n\\ndef format_as_chat(tokenizer, user_content: str, system_prompt: str = None) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Wrap content in chat template to match SFT training format.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    messages = []\\n    if system_prompt:\\n        messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"system\\\", \\\"content\\\": system_prompt})\\n    messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": user_content})\\n    return tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n        messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True\\n    )\\n\\n\\n\\n\\ndef extract_mcq_answer(text, valid_choices):\\n    text = text.strip()\\n    if not text:\\n        return None\\n    if text[0] in valid_choices:\\n        return text[0]\\n    match = re.search(r'(?:answer|Answer)\\\\s*(?:is|:)\\\\s*\\\\**\\\\s*([A-Z])', text)\\n    if match an\\n\\n...           per_item.append({\"index\": total - 1, \"correct\": is_correct, \"pred\": pred_letter, \"gold\": item[\"answer_letter\"]})\n    else:\n        for batch_start in tqdm(range(0, len(items), batch_size), desc=f\"Evaluating {benchmark}\"):\n            batch = items[batch_start:batch_start + batch_size]\n            prompts = [item[\"prompt\"] for item in batch]\n            choices_list = [item[\"choice_tokens\"] for item in batch]\n\n            batch_logprobs = batch_compute_choice_logprobs(model, tokenizer, prompts, choices_list)\n\n            for item, logprobs in zip(batch, batch_logprobs):\n                pred_idx = int(np.argmax(logprobs))\n                pred_letter = item[\"choice_tokens\"][pred_idx]\n                is_correct = pred_letter == item[\"answer_letter\"]\n                correct += int(is_correct)\n                total += 1\n                per_item.append({\"index\": total - 1, \"correct\": is_correct, \"pred\": pred_letter, \"gold\": item[\"answer_letter\"]})\n\n    accuracy = correct / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n    return {\"accuracy\": accuracy, \"correct\": correct, \"total\": total, \"per_item\": per_item}\n'''\nnew_lines.append(new_mcq)\n\n# 2 blank lines before format_mcq_example\nnew_lines.append('\\n')\nnew_lines.append('\\n')\n\n# Keep everything from format_mcq_example onwards\nnew_lines.extend(lines[format_mcq_start_line:])\n\nwith open(filepath, 'w') as f:\n    f.writelines(new_lines)\n\nprint(\"PATCH OK\")\nPATCHEOF\necho \"Script written\""
}
```

</details>

_source line 110 · 2026-05-18T12:52:33.579Z_

50/50 per-item 完全一致。

## 改造结果

**修改的函数**：
1. **新增 `batch_compute_choice_logprobs`** (L158-188)：接收一批 prompts + choices，left-pad tokenize → 单次 batch forward → 取每条 `logits[:, -1, :]` 计算 log_softmax → 返回每条的 choice logprobs
2. **改写 `evaluate_mcq`** (L190-283)：先预处理所有 items 到列表，非 generation 模式下按 `batch_size` 分批调用 `batch_compute_choice_logprobs`；generation 模式保持逐条不变

**关键 diff**：
- `tokenizer.padding_side = "left"` 保证 batch padding 正确（结束后恢复原值）
- `padding=True, truncation=True, max_length=2048` 用于 batch tokenize
- `logits[:, -1, :]` 取最后位置（left-pad 下所有序列的真实最后 token 都在末尾）
- `compute_choice_logprobs` 保留不动（`evaluate_paraphrased` 仍在使用）

**验证**：Qwen2.5-0.5B d000_s42, MMLU 50 items
- batch-size=16: accuracy=0.2200, 4 batch iterations
- batch-size=1: accuracy=0.2200, 50 iterations
- **Per-item match: 50/50**
